## 2026-10-06 ARV-113 Per-line minute counts in the stream (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1, PR #67)
- Summary: the queue engine counts every crossing per line and minute (entry, exit, overflow entry and a new Count role read from the published profile; interval counts spread as for the queue) in the pure `LineCounts` (Ariva.Core), closed once the watermark passes the minute and saved in the zone snapshot (`ZoneProcessorState` version 3). `StreamStore` writes them to `line_minute` (script 0037, hypertable, DELETE and TRUNCATE revoked, plus the read-only 15-minute aggregate `line_minute_15m`) by binary COPY into staging and an upsert in the checkpoint transaction. Source `Ariva` only (owner decision: `Vendor` deferred until Ariva computes crossings from tracks).
- Bound: at most 20,000 open line minutes per zone; beyond it the earliest is released early and later parts of that minute are written as additive parts (merged per checkpoint, added by a second constant upsert), so no count is lost. Known exception, documented: a commit that succeeds on the server but errors on the client and is retried adds an additive part twice; reachable only after an early release, which the engine cannot produce (only the watermark's minute is open, at most 1,000 lines per profile).
- Golden replay: new hash 8d13749079c0c0ca347dcd8c9e41f6708a7360b7145ad61af32c4d49cf0e0a2c (2,716 outputs; was 451f62ed547d4806ceb5e2e9587705cc67275442a4883f3b435021605ea11729, 2,083), approved by the owner on 2026-10-06: the replay now hashes 633 closed line minutes; every earlier output is unchanged (verified by leaving the line outputs out, which gives the old hash back). A replay of an old range now hashes differently from the value recorded in `replay_run` before this change.
- Tests: `LineCountsTests` (roles, count lines, interval spreading, late events inside and behind the watermark, restart mid-minute, the bound and its additive parts, hostile snapshots, the early-release mark), a golden check that line counts equal the zone's entries and exits per minute, `QueueStreamTests` (restart gives identical `line_minute` rows; store upserts, additive merging and adding, dropped rows logged by zone key; runtime role denied DELETE, TRUNCATE and writes to the aggregate).
- Gates: backend PASS (2,391 unit tests at first pass, then the fixes); integration 333 passed and 1 skipped; scan.mjs 0 errors; docs PASS.
- Security review: first pass FAIL (Medium CWE-120: an early-released minute could be overwritten and lose counts; Low: engine snapshot restore less strict, silent drops, aggregate privileges untested); fixed in c76221c; second pass PASS, its two Lows (document the retry exception, the early-release counter's comment) folded in.
- Learnings: a bound that releases state early must say what happens to the rest of that state; replace-on-upsert is idempotent only for whole values.


## 2026-10-06 ARV-114a Continuous health checks: conservation, track completion and occupancy sanity (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary: the pure `HealthChecks` (Ariva.Core/Queueing) computes F18's continuous checks per queue zone and bin, and `BinAccumulator` emits a `ZoneHealthBin` with every bin result (same start, revision, status). Inputs come from the queue engine: the queue's sensor occupancy at each minute boundary (queue zone plus the overflow bands heard so far, each fresh within 2 minutes; a band never heard counts as empty), each zone's lowest and highest reading per minute, tracked entries per minute and a `Tracked` flag on resolutions. Ariva.Api.Stream writes `zone_health_bin` (script 0038, hypertable, Final rows immutable, DELETE and TRUNCATE revoked) by binary COPY and upsert in the checkpoint transaction. Zones get an optional physical capacity (queue zones and overflow bands, 1 to 5,000, `zone.physical_capacity` in 0038), set on drafts, frozen with the published version, outside the geometry hash (F22 unchanged); the zone editor has a Physical capacity field; the demo v12 takes the scenario's snake capacities. `GET api/v1/sites/{siteCode}/zone-health?zone=&from=&to=` (new `DataQuality.View`: border shift supervisors, terminal duty managers, administrators) reads the latest revision of each bin, at most 31 days and 3,000 bins.
- Script 0038_zone_health.sql, SHA-256 b1c3a25277443f1000aacaf5a4bea62797b98435f4bd56718062bbe8127e8e31. `ZoneProcessorState` version 4 (bins open at the upgrade report only what follows it).
- Golden replay: new head 3cce63edfb17660f53a3fb7eead6c8ae92b20c491135b406a7df8cb032199a6f over 3,202 outputs (was 8d13749079c0c0ca347dcd8c9e41f6708a7360b7145ad61af32c4d49cf0e0a2c over 2,716): 486 health rows join the chain; `Replay_Should_KeepEveryEarlierOutput_When_TheHealthRowsAreLeftOut` rebuilds the chain without them and gets the ARV-113 head back. The in-memory reference replay now reads the evening's records in [From, To) as the archive does: the occupancy reading stamped exactly 20:30:00 changed no output before, but is an occupancy minute of the 20:30 bin now, and the archived replay (integration) left it out. Approved by the owner on 2026-10-06 (recorded in docs/product/decisions.md).
- Owner decisions (2026-10-06): the golden replay head above (3,202 outputs) is approved; the `DataQuality.View` grants as built (SystemAdministrator, BorderShiftSupervisor, TerminalDutyManager; not HandlerStationManager) are approved. Both recorded in docs/product/decisions.md.
- The reference evening is healthy: residual 0 in every bin with both ends measured, every track completes, no minute above capacity (the emulator's occupancy is exactly entries minus exits). Unit tests cover non-zero residuals, censored, fragmented, abandoned and open tracks, minutes above a zone's and a band's capacity, and the golden replay with CI-C at 40.
- Tests: `HealthChecksTests` (F18 table, engine samples and readings, tracked counts, bins, snapshots and hostile snapshots), zone capacity in `ZoneProfileTests`, the seed's capacities against the scenario's, golden checks; integration: restart gives identical `zone_health_bin` rows, store upsert, runtime role denied DELETE, TRUNCATE and changing a Final row, table checks, geometry source capacities; E2E: `zone-health.spec.ts` (reference evening values, latest revision, roles, cross-site 404 without data, ranges over 31 days 400, payloads), capacity payloads in `zone-profiles.spec.ts`, the field in `functional/zones.spec.ts`, permission matrix row.
- Gates: backend PASS (2,463 unit tests); integration 339 passed and 1 skipped (as before); web PASS; e2e 511 passed and 1 skipped; scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS.
- Security review: security-reviewer verdict PASS (no Critical/High/Medium; four Low findings fixed in this story: snapshot occupancy upper bounds, non-queue capacity E2E, parameterised test SQL, site-before-query E2E). The fixes: a restored engine refuses an occupancy sample above the queue's bound (`QueueZoneGeometry.MaxQueueOccupancy`, 10,000 per zone that counts in the queue) or of a minute twice, and a zone occupancy above 10,000; a restored bin refuses an opening or closing occupancy above the same bound (hostile rows in `HealthChecksTests`); `zone-profiles.spec.ts` sends a capacity for a service zone on adding and on editing (400 with the domain's message, never 500); the health row test in `QueueStreamTests` binds the bin start as a parameter; `zone-health.spec.ts` sends an invalid range to a site the caller does not reach and gets 404, not 400.
- Gates after the review fixes: backend PASS (2,468 unit tests); integration 339 passed and 1 skipped (as before); e2e 511 passed and 1 skipped (the new cases are assertions inside existing tests); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS.
- Learnings: a Playwright mouse drag needs its handle on screen: a taller side panel moved the plan's corner out of view after clicking Save, so the test scrolls the handle into view first. A replay's own range must match the archive's, or an input at the boundary that changed nothing yet will change a later output; a health input must never mark a bin changed, or it adds bin results and changes earlier outputs.

## 2026-10-06 ARV-115 Overflow band occupancy, OverflowDetected and the OverflowOccupied rule (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary: the pure `OverflowBands` (Ariva.Core/Queueing) takes each overflow band's readings from the engine's steps (`QueueStep.Readings`), holds the lowest and highest per minute until the watermark passes the minute, and releases an `OverflowMinute`; each closed minute moves the band's state, so `OverflowChange` Occupied (first occupied minute after an empty or unheard spell) and Emptied (first minute with readings and no occupancy after an occupied spell) come once each; a minute without readings changes nothing. `ZoneProcessorState` version 5 keeps the open band minutes and band states (a version 4 snapshot restores with every band empty); version 6 adds a band's Unknown state (owner's silent band decision below; a version 5 snapshot restores as written). `StreamStore` writes `overflow_minute` (script 0039, hypertable, DELETE and TRUNCATE revoked; parts of a minute released early merge by LEAST and GREATEST) by binary COPY and upsert, and the `OverflowDetected` event (Ariva.Core/Domain/Events, `[KafkaTopic(FlowOverflowDetected)]`, keyed by the zone key) into `outbox_message` in the same checkpoint transaction, with a name-based id (zone, band, minute, change, profile version) and ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING, so a repeated checkpoint adds no second event. View `overflow_bin_15m` counts overflow minutes per zone and 15-minute bin (TC-19, Proposed). `AlertInputs` supplies `OverflowOccupied` (1 when any watched band held anyone in the minute, 0 when the bands reported empty, nothing without a reading; a queue zone watches all its bands, an overflow zone its own) and `LatestOverflowMinutesAsync` for the live tick; `OverflowOccupied` left `AlertRuleErrors.NotEvaluatedMetrics` and the web's `notEvaluated`, so R-002 is evaluated.
- Script 0039_overflow_minute.sql, SHA-256 4cb65ba8c39acf6bda350b962dc0e07e577acc87f972cb65bbe64ba5bfc64829 (was f2664569d05baff7d04b7508d358c7b1df0e38ed92472d29b23a4b7d92b755e2 before its comment recorded TC-19 as decided; the script is not merged yet, so it changed with checksums.lock).
- Scenario: seed 9303's reference evening never fills a band (A-VIS peaks at 148 of 190), so the simulator gained the case `ScenarioConfig.OverflowEvening` (Visitors snake 60 people, scripted events unchanged) and the sensor emulator splits a queue at the run's snake capacity (`ScenarioDay.SnakeCapacity`, the defaults unless set). In the case A-OV is occupied 18:03 to 18:31 and 19:33 to 19:52 (49 overflow minutes; S-25 is the band's lead).
- Golden replay: unchanged, 3cce63edfb17660f53a3fb7eead6c8ae92b20c491135b406a7df8cb032199a6f over 3,202 outputs (no band reports in the reference evening, so no overflow output joins the chain; `Replay_Should_KeepItsGoldenHashUnchanged_When_NoBandIsEverOccupied`). New locked value for the overflow case in `Ariva.UnitTests/Replay/replay-overflow.json`: d971a3170539758f5248bfcc01e181e3bfd9ab1aa51dfba638d8908be9948b9a over 1,692 outputs (209 band minutes, 4 band changes), reproduced by the archived replay in integration; approved by the owner on 2026-10-06 and unchanged by the fixes below (re-run: same head, same 1,692 outputs; the reference golden head is unchanged too, 3,202 outputs).
- Owner decisions (Ahmad Al-Sadder, 2026-10-06; docs/product/decisions.md, the Open decisions row closed): the locked overflow replay value is approved; TC-19 (any band's highest reading above zero; no reading means not observed) is accepted; a silent band becomes Unknown after the freshness window. Silent band as built: with L a band's latest minute with readings, the band becomes Unknown at minute L + 3 (L plus one minute plus `OccupancyFreshFor`, 2 minutes, rounded up to a whole minute) when minutes L + 1 to L + 3 have no reading, decided when the watermark passes that minute's end, in minute and then band order among the band minutes (`OverflowBands.Silence`); `OverflowDetected` gets state Unknown (the event's `State` is now the enum `OverflowChangeKind`, written by name, so the AsyncAPI schema lists Occupied, Emptied, Unknown); the next reading raises Occupied, or Emptied with a peak of 0. A band never heard is not Unknown, and a replay's end is not a silence (`ZoneProcessor.Finish` stops it as it stops device outages), which is why the overflow case's hash is unchanged: without that, settling past 20:30 made A-OV Unknown at 20:32 (one extra output, head cf6040ab34e50dc2c66c22132484e9938777d35b7b30e6aed60faa41d97cf746 over 1,693, never adopted). `OverflowOccupied` needed no code change: it reads `overflow_minute` rows only, so an Unknown band adds neither a 1 nor a 0 (documented in AlertInputs, formulas F17, wiki 10 and 11).
- Tests: `OverflowBandsTests` (min and max per minute, open minute revised inside the lateness, each change once over two spells, silent band, foreign zones and impossible readings ignored, exact continuation through JSON snapshots, the open bound and early release, 19 hostile snapshots, the bound on restore, a pre-ARV-115 snapshot, ZoneProcessor drain and hostile state); `OverflowDetectedTests` (fields, key, topic, name-based id, refused keys, payload names); `OverflowReplayTests` (the case's band minutes, changes and per-bin counts, the locked hash, the stream with snapshots and checkpoints at uneven intervals equals the replay, queue length from both zones); golden check. Integration: `QueueStreamTests` (Q1 with a band: restart writes the same `overflow_minute` rows and the same outbox events; store rewrite adds no event, parts merge, bin view; runtime role cannot delete, truncate, break the checks or write the view; privileges), `OverflowAlertTests` (R-002 raised 18:05 and 19:35, cleared 18:34 and 19:55; a rule on A-OV raised 18:03 and 19:33; backtest equals live for both), `GoldenReplayArchiveTests` (archived overflow case gives the locked hash), `AlertRuleServiceTests` (an OverflowOccupied rule is accepted; DesksBelowPlan still refused). E2E: `functional/alert-rules.spec.ts` (R-002 shows no "Not evaluated yet", the form offers OverflowOccupied, the server refuses DesksBelowPlan). AsyncAPI document and wiki 08 table regenerated (producer api-stream). Added after the first review: `OverflowBandsTests` (no change within the window; `UnknownFrom` for 2 minutes, 90 s, 1 minute, 1 tick and 5 minutes; occupied then silent becomes Unknown at minute 4, not a tick before the watermark passes it; empty then silent, recovery to Emptied, silent again, then Occupied; a sensor reporting every third minute never flaps; one step or many give the same changes in the same order; Unknown through JSON and its recovery; a version 5 band state without the property; six hostile Unknown states; ZoneProcessor: time alone makes a band Unknown, a version 5 snapshot restores and goes Unknown like any other, an occupied Unknown state is refused, a replay reports silences inside its range only), `OverflowDetectedTests` (Unknown written by name, round trip, id differs by change), `TopicTests` (the NHibernate outbox refuses a 201-character key with its reason and passes 200; `OutboxLimits` counts code points), `QueueStreamTests.Store_Should_DropWhatTheTablesWouldRefuseAndCommit_When_ACheckpointCarriesInvalidOverflowRows` (a 218-character zone key: change dropped with exactly one warning, minute written; empty and 201-character band names, Min above Max, Min below 0, Max above 10,000, a peak above 10,000, zone keys `Q1` and `/Q1` without a site; nothing written, no outbox row, the checkpoint's offset committed, exact warnings without band names), `OverflowAlertTests.Inputs_Should_ReadTheSitesOwnBandsOnly_When_AnotherSiteHasZonesAndBandsOfTheSameNames` (XXX/A-VIS with bands A-OV and A-VIS occupied every minute and past DMO's last: DMO's series, latest band minute and R-002 backtest unchanged; a zone outside DMO's profile gets an empty series).
- Gates (first run): backend PASS (2,510 unit tests); integration 348 passed and 1 skipped; web PASS; e2e 511 passed and 1 skipped; scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS.
- Security review (CWE-501, CWE-862), first verdict FAIL: one Medium, two Low, two notes. Fixed: (Medium) a zone key over the outbox's 200-character `message_key` (a 17-character site and a 200-character zone make 218) failed the checkpoint insert with 22001, which `QueueStreamWorker` treats as a database outage and retries forever; now `OutboxLimits.MaxMessageKeyLength` (Ariva.Infra/Messaging/Outbox) is checked by `StreamStore` (the change is dropped with one warning, the band minute is still written) and by `NHibernateDomainEventOutbox` (the commit fails with the reason, not 22001). (Low) the store's drop paths were untested; tests added, and band minutes above 10,000 are now dropped too (the table's check would have refused them), as are changes with a peak out of bounds. (Low) no cross-site test of the overflow inputs; added. (Notes) ADR-0018 consequences name `StreamStore` as a second outbox writer (same transaction, published by the relay after commit); the ASVS V2.3.3 evidence names the QueueStreamTests (row stays Met). Re-review verdict PASS (security-reviewer, 2026-10-06): all three findings fixed and tested, the silent band Unknown state checked for CWE-501 and CWE-120, no new finding at Medium or above. Two observations carried forward: a functional test that R-002 neither clears nor grows through an Unknown spell (add with ARV-114b), and a queue-zone target counting rows of bands removed from the current profile within the rule window (note for the KPI annex).
- Deferred: `ZoneHealthChanged` through NHibernate has the same latent key overflow: the shared check gives a clear error, but the commit still fails, so the device health sweep would fail on every pass for such a zone; backlog ARV-114c (prd-phase1.json, priority after ARV-115) removes the cause (a profile rule or a wider key) and keeps the sweep going. Flagging zone health for a dead overflow band sensor (the band going Unknown) is not done here; a candidate for ARV-114b's alarms.
- Gates after the review fixes: backend PASS (2,539 unit tests); integration 350 passed and 1 skipped; web PASS; e2e 511 passed and 1 skipped (no web or API change in the fixes; run because the story touched the alert rules spec); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS.
- Learnings: the emulator stamps a minute's occupancy at the minute's end, so the band minute that holds a reading is the one after the scenario minute it describes; a view that aggregates refuses writes with 55000 before the privilege check, so test its grants with has_table_privilege.

## 2026-10-06 ARV-114c Zone keys that fit the outbox: ZoneHealthChanged and every event keyed by zone (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary: the default path (validation, not a wider column). The 200-character key limit moved to Core as `MessageKeys` (Ariva.Core/Messaging: `MaxLength`, `Fits` counting code points as PostgreSQL does); `OutboxLimits` in Infra now follows it (`MaxMessageKeyLength = MessageKeys.MaxLength`, `FitsMessageKey` delegates), so Core never references Infra. `ZoneKeys` gained `Fits(site, queueZoneName)` and `MaxQueueZoneNameLength(site)`. `ZoneProfile.AddZone` and `RenameZone` refuse a queue zone name whose key would pass 200 characters (`ZoneProfile.QueueZoneNameTooLong(site)`: names the site and the allowed length, never the refused name; 400 through the existing domain error path), and `Validate` (so publishing too) lists such a zone in a draft copied from an earlier version. Only queue zone names are bound: every zone-keyed event (the sensing batches, `DeviceHealthReported`, `ZoneHealthChanged`, `OverflowDetected`) uses `<site>/<queue zone name>`, devices bind only to queue zones, and service, staff and overflow zones share their queue zone's key (the band name rides in `OverflowDetected`'s payload). Site codes never change, so nothing else can push a key over. `SvcDeviceHealth.AssessZoneAsync` skips a zone whose key does not fit (one warning per zone per process through `DeviceHealthMetrics.ZoneNotKeyable`, counter `ariva.zones.not_keyable`), so the sweep and the health consumer commit the other zones and the device states instead of failing every pass.
- Validation messages are English domain messages like the other zone profile rules: `Ariva.Resources/Languages` holds no validation resources yet, so there was no Arabic resource to add.
- Seeds: `DemoTopologySeedTests.BuildProfile_Should_GiveEveryQueueZoneAKeyThatFitsAMessageKey_When_Built` (DMO's longest queue zone name is 5 characters; the seed builds through `AddZone`, so it would fail loudly anyway). No other seed adds zones. Migration note for deployed profiles: wiki 10 section 4.2a (SQL to find the zones and devices, then draft, rename, publish, move and recalibrate the devices).
- Tests: unit `ZoneProfileTests` (key of exactly 200 accepted; 201 refused without repeating the name; 7 astral characters and 186 letters, 200 UTF-16 units and 193 characters, accepted, 6 and 188 refused; rename refused and the old name kept; overflow, service and staff names of 200 accepted; a copied overlong queue zone is a validation problem and blocks publishing), `TopicTests` (`OutboxLimits` follows `MessageKeys`; `ZoneKeys.Fits` at DMO, AUH-T1 and a 17-character site, ASCII and astral, null parts), `ZoneHealthTests` (`ZoneNotKeyable` counts every skip, true once per zone), `DemoTopologySeedTests` (above). Integration `DeviceHealthTests`: `ZoneHealthChanged_Should_ReachTheOutbox_When_TheZoneKeyIsTheLongestAllowed` (HBF and a 196-character name through the service: Healthy then Degraded in the outbox with a 200-character `message_key`), `Sweep_Should_CommitTheOtherZonesAndTheDeviceChanges_When_OneZoneKeyIsTooLongForTheOutbox` (a device row moved to a 200-character zone name by SQL, a 204-character key: the sweep commits Snake A's events and both devices' Offline marks, writes nothing for the overlong zone, and a later report and sweep still commit; with the skip removed the test fails with the outbox's "partition key longer than 200 characters"). E2E `zone-profiles.spec.ts`: at E2E2, 195 letters and 5 astral plus 190 letters are accepted; 196 and 200 letters, 4 astral plus 192, and markup and SQL payloads padded to 196 answer 400 without any fragment of the name; a rename to 196 answers 400 and leaves Snake A.
- Docs: docs/product/decisions.md (decision proposed by the engineer, pending the owner, and an Open decisions row), wiki 10 section 4.2a, wiki 11 section 7 (name rule), docs/architecture/overview.md (zone key limit).
- Gates: backend PASS (2,554 unit tests); integration 352 passed and 1 skipped (full); e2e 511 passed and 1 skipped (full run, fresh services); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS.
- Security review (CWE-120, CWE-501): security-reviewer verdict PASS (2026-10-06), no blocking finding. Folded in: a unit test pinning that a lone surrogate counts as one character (Low), and a runbook 4.2a line to alert on `ariva.zones.not_keyable` since skipped zones are not in `ariva.zones.degraded_now` (Info). Left as is (Info): `Validate`'s problem text names the zone, which is stored data shown only to administrators, JSON encoded, like the other problem texts.
- Learnings: the device health sweep is deployment-wide, so integration tests on the shared database assert at least the devices they own were marked; the name rule counts UTF-16 units (200) while the key rule counts code points, so an astral name can be refused by either, and a test of code point counting needs a name of exactly 200 units.

## 2026-10-06 ARV-116 Staff and service zone occupancy into the desk engine (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary: a staff or service zone of the published profile that names its desk (not an e-gate) now feeds that desk's state at its F10 rank. `ZoneGeometrySource` links each such zone hanging off a queue zone to the desk's key (site/checkpoint/desk, `DeskKeys`; one zone per desk and role, the first by name; keys over 64 characters left out) in `QueueZoneGeometry.DeskZones`. The zone processor passes their occupancy on as `DeskZoneSample` (desk key, role, time, count, the sensing pipeline's flag; counts only) through the pure `DeskZoneReadings` (Ariva.Core/Desks), never into the queue; any other zone's occupancy that is not the queue's is now counted (`ZoneProcessorCounters.OtherZones`) and kept from the queue engine, where a late one counted as a late event of the bins. `StreamStore` writes the samples to `desk_zone_reading` (script 0040) by binary COPY and an insert that keeps the first row of a (desk, role, time) in the checkpoint transaction. `DeskFeed` reads every site with AMAN desks or desk zones (`SitesAsync`), configures AMAN desks with their zones and desks with zones and no AMAN code with the zones alone, and reads the new readings by write time (`AmanFeedCursor.Take<T>`, the same overlap and memory; `DeskFeedState.ZoneCursor`, absent in older states). `ZoneProcessorState` version 7 (`DeskZones`: the latest reading passed on per desk zone); a version 6 snapshot restores with none.
- Engine changes (F10 and F11, Proposed, pending the owner): `DeskZoneReading.Degraded` (a flagged reading degrades the state while it is the zone's latest; kept in buffered snapshot signals and `DeskMemory`); `DeskStateSettings.SensorPauseAfter` (T1 without a live login source; null keeps T1; the desk feed sets 60 s through `Border:DeskFeed:SensorPauseSeconds`, 10 to 180); a staff or service zone counts as a configured source only once heard (the XS2 probe and real profiles draw service zones only to link queues to desks); a restored snapshot refuses a buffered signal the engine would refuse (a zone role the desk lacks, a count above 50).
- Emulator: S-18, S-19 and S-20 (A-VIS slots 3 to 5, heartbeat-only before) report the staff and service zones of AR-08 to AR-22, five desks each (`SensorRole.DeskZones`; staff 1 while serving or idle, service 1 while serving, read at the minute's start; a desk the scenario marks unknown is not reported). Seed 9303's scripted events and the queue and band sensors' traffic are unchanged. The demo profile v12 is unchanged, so the demo does not register these sensors (decision pending).
- Scenario (`DeskZoneScenarioTests`, the reference evening through Ingest, the A-VIS zone processor and a desk engine with the feed's settings and sensor-only Visitors desks): open desks within one desk of the scenario in 206 of 209 minutes from 17:01 to 20:29 (98.6 percent; Proposed bound 95 percent, recorded in docs/product/decisions.md pending the owner), 87.1 percent exact, largest difference 5 (19:30, five desks closing together). With the 3-minute T1 it was 93.7 percent, hence the sensor T1. Silencing S-19 from 18:30 to 18:45 turns its five desks Unknown and Degraded from 18:31 to 18:44 and changes no other desk minute. Replaying A-VIS with S-18 to S-20 added gives the golden replay's A-VIS outputs exactly, plus 6,296 desk readings (desk case head 807253ec2cbf4d4be460e58bba939cd048d223c5b7240bb18aa5a91910792a64 over 7,776 outputs, not locked), and twice gives the same hashes.
- Golden replay: unchanged, 3cce63edfb17660f53a3fb7eead6c8ae92b20c491135b406a7df8cb032199a6f over 3,202 outputs (no desk zones in the reference geometry; the ledger writes `desk-reading` outputs only when a zone has them). The overflow case is unchanged too.
- Script 0040_desk_zone_reading.sql, SHA-256 246ab5abf425cc0407763b9b7517b401865e57f5db38b996eac5743d5a44944a: hypertable on reading time (1-day chunks, 7-day retention, Proposed), primary key (desk, role, time), index (site, write time), checks (role, occupancy 0 to 50, desk key starting with its site code); the runtime role inserts and reads only (UPDATE, DELETE and TRUNCATE revoked).
- Tests: unit `DeskZoneTests` (counts only and no track id from mixed crossings, tracks and desk zones; the filter's order, keep-alive, invalid counts, the per-minute bound under a 100,000-reading flood and a 90,000-reading flood through the zone processor; exact continuation through JSON, 8 hostile memories, the 1,000-entry bound, renamed desks forgotten; queue outputs unchanged by desk zones; version 6 snapshots; engine: sensor-only serve, idle, pause after 60 s and close, logged-in T1 unchanged, AMAN's rank, never-heard zones, flagged and silent readings in the minutes, the per-desk and per-engine bounds under a 50,000-reading flood with another desk still served, hostile buffered zone signals, the flag through a snapshot, the setting's bounds), `DeskFeedZoneTests` (role names exact, the generic cursor, old feed states, settings), `DeskZoneScenarioTests` (above), `SensorTrafficTests` (the new role and its readings; Ingest's names include the desk zones for these sensors). Integration: `QueueStreamTests` (Q1 with a desk: the restart writes exactly the same 200 readings; the store keeps the first row and its id on a repeated checkpoint, drops 7 invalid readings with one warning per zone naming no desk and commits; the runtime role cannot update, delete or truncate the table or break its checks), `DeskZoneFeedTests` (XS3: a sensor-only desk Serving then Idle, sensor-derived, then Unknown and Degraded; an AMAN desk logged out stays Closed with presence recorded, then falls back to its zones, Degraded, when AMAN is silent; an e-gate's zone and a plain desk stay out; XS4 with no AMAN desk is a feed site and gets minutes).
- Gates: backend PASS (2,612 unit tests; the first run failed `OverflowBandsTests` on the snapshot version, fixed, and once `AmanFeedPactTests` on a race in its output collector, which passed on rerun and in the second full run); integration PASS (360 passed and 1 skipped, as before); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS. E2E not run: no API or web change (no E2E test registers S-18 to S-20).
- Security review (CWE-501, CWE-120): first verdict FAIL on documentation only (no code change): `desk_zone_reading` is desk-level border data (staff presence per desk) and had no row in the retention tables. Fixed: a row in docs/domain/data-boundary.md and wiki/14 section 3 (7 days, stays in the border deployment, no API reads it). Every CWE and ASVS row the diff touches passes; with the rows added the reviewer's verdict is PASS. Notes carried forward: a device may stamp a reading up to 5 minutes ahead, which hides that desk zone's later readings until then (same trust a device already has over its queue counts; consider capping at receipt time plus a tolerance); `DeskFeedState.ZoneCursor` is not range-checked on load (as the AMAN cursor; size capped). Owner decisions 2026-10-07: the 95 percent bound and the 60-second sensor T1 kept as Proposed, both re-measured in the pilot validation (ARV-104); desk zones go into the AUH seed (ARV-139a).
- Deferred: a demo profile with the Visitors desk zones (owner decision); the shadow nowcast's sensor-only desk term (ARV-117): a sensor-only immigration desk now writes `desk_minute`, so `DeskTermSource` counts it in n_open, while its cycle time needs AMAN's statistics or transactions; locking the desk case's replay value if wanted.
- Learnings: the 7-day retention job drops a test's fixed past dates within seconds of the script, so tests on such tables date their rows now or remove the policy; a profile zone is not a sensor until it reports.

## 2026-10-07 ARV-117 Sensor-only shadow nowcast for the ground-truth proof (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary: every live queue minute now carries a shadow nowcast without AMAN inputs, written in the same `queue_minute` row and checkpoint as the published one (script 0041_shadow_nowcast.sql, SHA-256 00f21ae0fa1c6822cd5355041c528131110ab16e5d6f8b6feeee08101258777f after the second review fix (efbe46fe46c76986faee4621d66da4d56cd1dab08da9dc254df139d064c6c388 after the first, 145bd806fdaabffffb4255b83901f594dff945ac5ba70acb76eb8ffbcdc68ba6 before; not merged, so still editable): `shadow_nowcast_minutes`, `shadow_no_service`, `shadow_nowcast_degraded`; checks: minutes at least 0, reason one of the `NoServiceReason` names, never a number and a reason together, and the F11 flag exactly when there is a number or a reason). `ShadowNowcasts.Inputs` (Ariva.Core/Queueing/ShadowNowcast.cs) builds both inputs from one queue-side input (Q, exits, window, the queue's flags) and a desk term: the published one takes the term as before (its row is unchanged), the shadow its `DeskTerm.SensorOnly` part. `DeskTerms.SensorOnly`: n_open counts desks open for at least half of the term's minute by their zones alone (`desk_minute.sensor_derived_seconds`, now read by `DeskTermSource` into `DeskMinuteSample.SensorDerivedSeconds`); a desk open through AMAN only is Unknown to the shadow (flags it; with none open from the zones there is no term); no cycle time. `QueueLiveMinute.Shadow` is `[JsonIgnore]`, so neither the live snapshot nor the replay ledger carries it. Computed always: no setting turns it off.
- Decisions (docs/product/decisions.md; points (1) and (2) decided by the owner, Ahmad Al-Sadder, on 2026-10-07: keep the F8 exit-term fallback for the cycle time, flagged Degraded, and revisit with pilot data; AMAN-only desks Unknown to the shadow accepted for ARV-117 so it runs from the first day of burn-in, with the follow-up ARV-117a, a sensor-only desk engine per site, added to backlog/prd-phase1.json at priority 7 (later priorities shifted by one), depending on ARV-117, and made a dependency of ARV-137 so the go-live gate cannot pass without it; it must land before burn-in at a site where every desk has an AMAN code). As proposed: (1) the sensor desk term has no cycle time without AMAN (AMAN's statistics and the desk minutes' transactions are both AMAN), so the shadow takes the F8 fallback: the exit term alone, flagged, or NothingOpen when the zones show every desk closed; alternative offered: c = sensor open desk minutes / queue exits over the window. (2) A desk open through AMAN only is Unknown to the shadow. Limitation: with AMAN live the desk engine records no sensor-derived time for AMAN desks, so at an all-AMAN site (AUH) the shadow is the exit term alone while AMAN is live; a sensor-only desk engine per site would be a follow-up story. (3) No column-level SELECT revoke; the absent read path is enforced by tests.
- Golden replay: unchanged, 3cce63edfb17660f53a3fb7eead6c8ae92b20c491135b406a7df8cb032199a6f over 3,202 outputs (a replay has no desk term, so every replayed minute's shadow equals its published nowcast, tested; the ledger never serialises it). Overflow case unchanged too.
- Tests: unit `ShadowNowcastTests` (the F8 cases: 7.5 and 7.89 published against the exit term alone 8.33 or NoThroughputData in the shadow; NothingOpen in both; AMAN-only desks; 500 random inputs differ only in n_open, c and the desk flag; 400 random desk sets: the shadow never moves when AMAN's cycle time, transactions or the published n_open change, and equals the published nowcast with no AMAN input; the zone writes the shadow beside an unchanged published row; JSON of the ledger, the snapshot and plain serialisation never contain it), `ShadowNowcastExposureTests` (CWE-862, CWE-863: the field graph of every Ariva assembly reaches the shadow only from the engine, its outputs and the stream's checkpoint, worker and store; the live snapshot, `Ariva.Infra.Live`, the hub, displays, alerting, reports, view models, controllers and the AMAN contracts hold none and have no member named after it; only StreamStore.cs and the script name its columns, only the engine and the store name the value), `GoldenReplayTests` (above), `OverflowReplayTests` (compares the ledger's rows without the shadow). Integration: `QueueStreamTests` (a store test: both nowcasts in one row, a later checkpoint replaces both, the checks refuse a forged shadow; the restart test compares the shadow columns too and every live row's shadow equals its published nowcast), `ImmigrationIntakeTests` (the sensor-only term from `desk_minute`: none while the desks are AMAN-only, then one desk, flagged, no cycle time).
- Gates (first run): backend PASS (2,624 unit tests); integration PASS (361 passed and 1 skipped; 360 before plus the new store test); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS. E2E not run: no API or web change.
- Security review, first verdict: FAIL (one Medium, two Low, one ASVS note). Fixed: (Medium, CWE-862 and CWE-863 test gap) `ShadowNowcastExposureTests` now fails on any production .cs or .sql statement that pairs `queue_minute` (whole word, so `stage_queue_minute` and `queue_minute_15m` are not it) with `SELECT *`, `RETURNING *`, `<alias>.*`, `row_to_json`, `to_json` or `to_jsonb`, `json_agg` or `jsonb_agg` of a bare alias, or `COPY queue_minute`; a statement ends at a semicolon (the SQL statement, or the C# statement holding the command text however it is built), comments removed, so StreamStore's staging wildcards on other tables pass; a theory proves the rule on 18 statements in SQL and C# form; the value regex also matches `Shadow:` property patterns and named arguments. New integration test `QueueStreamTests.Migrations_Should_LeaveNoViewOverTheShadowColumns_When_Applied` (database it_stream_shadow_views): after the migrations no view, materialized view or continuous aggregate depends on a `shadow_` column of `queue_minute` (`pg_depend` through `pg_rewrite`, with a positive control that `queue_minute_15m`'s internal views are seen); it bites: a `SELECT *` view and a materialized view naming `shadow_nowcast_minutes`, created in a transaction, are both reported, then rolled back. (Low) `Script0041_Should_AllowExactlyTheNoServiceReasons_When_ItsCheckIsParsed` compares `Enum.GetNames<NoServiceReason>()` with the IN list parsed from 0041. (Low) 0041 gains `ck_queue_minute_shadow_flag`, `(minutes IS NULL AND reason IS NULL) OR degraded IS NOT NULL`, with two forged updates (a number, a reason, each without the flag) in the store test's 23514 loop; checksums.lock updated. (ASVS) V8.1.2 evidence records the field-level rule for the `shadow_*` columns (row stays Partly), and ARV-104f's notes require its own read role or permissioned endpoint with authorization tests.
- Gates after the fixes: backend PASS (2,645 unit tests); integration PASS (362 passed and 1 skipped); scan.mjs 0 errors; docs PASS; quick PASS. Re-review requested.
- Security re-review: FAIL (one Medium, two Low). Fixed: (Medium, CWE-862 and CWE-863) a whole-row reference to `queue_minute` (`row_to_json(q)`, `SELECT q`, `jsonb_agg(q ORDER BY ...)`) records only refobjsubid 0 in `pg_depend`, so the shadow-column filter missed it. Database side: `QueueStreamTests.Migrations_Should_LeaveNoViewOverTheShadowColumns_When_Applied` now lists every rewrite rule (view, materialized view, continuous aggregate internal view, rule) and every SQL-standard function that depends on `queue_minute` or one of its chunks (`pg_inherits`) at any refobjsubid, and asserts equality with an exact allowlist; Timescale's generated internal view names (`_partial_view_N`, `_direct_view_N`) are named through `_timescaledb_catalog.continuous_agg`, so the captured set (pg17, TimescaleDB 2.30) is `public.queue_minute_15m (continuous aggregate direct view)` and `(continuous aggregate partial view)`. The positive control in the rolled-back transaction adds a `row_to_json(q)` view, a `SELECT q` view, a `jsonb_agg(q ORDER BY q.minute_utc)` view, a SQL function returning `json_agg(q)` and a `to_jsonb` view over one of the hypertable's chunks; each is caught (hypertables refuse rules, so no rule control). Source side: `ShadowNowcastExposureTests` collects, per statement, the aliases declared for `queue_minute` (schema prefix and double quotes allowed; keywords after the table are not aliases, and a missing keyword only adds findings) and flags any use of an alias, or of the bare table name outside a table-reference position (after FROM, JOIN, INTO, UPDATE, TABLE, ONLY, COPY, ON, EXISTS, LATERAL), that is not `.column` and not the declaration (`SELECT q`, `f(q)`, `f(q ORDER BY ...)`, `(q).*`, `q::text`, `json_build_object('r', q)`, `array_agg(q)`, `q IS NOT NULL`; parameters `:q` and `@q` and string literals are not uses; a comma-separated table list naming `queue_minute` is flagged, write a JOIN); it also flags the `TABLE queue_minute` command (not ALTER, CREATE, LOCK TABLE or ON TABLE) and `COPY` with a schema prefix or quotes. The theory grows to 47 statements, each in SQL and C# form (23 new flagged cases, 6 new negatives beside the 6 existing ones); current production sources pass. Accepted residual risk (also in the V8.1.2 evidence): the scan is per statement, so SQL assembled across separate C# statements, or at run time, is not seen; the exact-dependents allowlist (database objects) and ARV-104f's dedicated read role are the backstops. (Low) `Nowcast.Compute` returns a number or a reason on every path, never neither (new unit test `Compute_Should_GiveExactlyANumberOrAReason_When_InputsAreAnything`, 5,000 random inputs with negative, NaN, infinite and missing values), so `ck_queue_minute_shadow_flag` is now `(shadow_nowcast_minutes IS NULL AND shadow_no_service IS NULL) = (shadow_nowcast_degraded IS NULL)` and the 23514 loop gains the forged flag without a shadow; `ShadowNowcast` is a public record, so StreamStore writes the flag only beside a number or a reason (a hand-built shadow with neither becomes no shadow instead of failing the checkpoint; covered in the store test). New 0041 SHA-256 in checksums.lock. (Low) The column regex is now case-insensitive `shadow_?(nowcast|no_?service)` (StreamStore.cs, the script, and the engine's ShadowNowcast.cs and ZoneProcessor.cs match it), and the more robust guard was added too: `Members_Should_BeNamedAfterTheShadowOnlyOnTheWritePath_When_EveryArivaTypeIsReflected` fails on any member (including compiler-generated ones) named after the shadow on any Ariva type outside the engine and the stream's checkpoint, worker and store, so a convention-mapped `ShadowNowcastMinutes` on an entity is caught however its name was written. docs/product/decisions.md and the V8.1.2 evidence describe the widened checks.
- Gates after the second fixes: backend PASS (2,676 unit tests); integration PASS (362 passed and 1 skipped); scan.mjs 0 errors (6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS.
- Third security review (2026-10-07): verdict PASS, no blocking finding. Confirmed the runtime role cannot create views, functions, triggers or publications (0001_roles.sql; SqlScriptRunnerTests), so only versioned migrations can add a read path; plpgsql bodies in migrations are seen by the source scan. Accepted residual risk: SQL assembled across separate C# statements (backstops: the exact dependents allowlist and ARV-104f's own read role) and session-local temp views. Optional hardening carried forward: assert no user trigger on queue_minute and no publication of it. A domain question from the review (a desk term without a sensor-only part falling back to the exit term without the shadow's flag) does not arise: `Nowcast.Compute` flags every exit-only result Degraded (F8 sensor-outage fallback), as the owner's decision of 2026-10-07 requires. Structural alternative offered to the owner: move the shadow to its own table the runtime login cannot read (same transaction), so the database enforces the rule instead of tests; pending the owner's choice, for ARV-117a.
- Deferred: the validation comparison that reads the shadow (ARV-104f, with its own authorized read path); a sensor-only desk engine for AMAN desks (ARV-117a); a sensor cycle time (revisit with pilot data).

## 2026-10-07 ARV-118 Availability ledger per operating minute (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary: a site operating calendar and a write-once availability ledger for the pilot criterion "availability 99 percent of operating hours" (D6, formulas F18, Proposed). Core (`Ariva.Core/Availability`, pure): `OperatingInterval` and `WeeklyHours` ("HH:MM" intervals per weekday in site local time, at most 4 a day, overnight intervals, no overlap on the week's circle, canonical JSON read back strictly), `OperatingCalendar` (per UTC minute: Operating, Closed or Maintenance with its local date; weekly versions, dated exceptions and maintenance windows each count only when recorded before they take effect), `AvailabilityRule` (thresholds in `Availability:*`: grace 120 s, live window 180 s, staleness 150 s, lag 180 s; live decision from Redis snapshots and queue_minute rows; catch-up from the database alone) and `AvailabilitySummary` (ratio, days, Monday-to-Sunday weeks, range). Entities `OperatingWeek`, `OperatingDayException`, `MaintenanceWindow` (NHibernate, soft deleted, `ISiteBound`). Ariva.Api.Main: `api/v1/admin/sites/{siteCode}/calendar` (GET with ViewSite; PUT weeks, POST exceptions and maintenance-windows, DELETE each with EditSite; `[SiteScoped]`; audited `SiteCalendar.*`; 404 for another site or another site's entry, 409 once an entry has taken effect or for a second exception of a day) and `GET api/v1/sites/{siteCode}/availability?from=&to=` (DataQuality.View, local dates, at most 92 days; 404 for a foreign site before the range is checked; 400 without echo). Ariva.Api.Cronz: TickerQ `AvailabilityLedger` every minute at second 30 (`AvailabilityLedger` in Ariva.Infra/Availability): per site with a published or retired profile, one transaction under `pg_try_advisory_xact_lock(50, hashtext(site))`, live minutes first, then the backlog from `availability_cursor` (at most 1,440 minutes a run), binary COPY into a staging table and INSERT ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING; Redis and the database only (a unit test checks the ledger takes no HTTP client); after a Redis failure the rest of the run does not wait for Redis again.
- Script 0042_availability.sql, SHA-256 68daa57e718b00714984dd13adff98b989c0eb3b771ae47df7db94c45ab2090e: `operating_week`, `operating_day_exception`, `maintenance_window` (runtime role: no DELETE, TRUNCATE), `availability_minute` (hypertable, 30-day chunks, PK site and minute, checks on states, reasons and zone counts; runtime role: no UPDATE, DELETE, TRUNCATE) and `availability_cursor`.
- Decisions taken (Proposed, recorded in docs/product/decisions.md with an Open decisions row, TC-83 in docs/product/pilot-to-confirm.md): (1) available minute as in the story's proposal, made concrete: every queue zone of the profile in force at the minute has a Redis snapshot published within 150 s whose minute has reached the minute and trails real time by at most 180 s, and a queue_minute row; (2) a maintenance window recorded at or after its start does not count at all (the API refuses it, the calendar ignores such a row), not "from its recorded time", so an outage cannot be excused once it has begun; weeks and exceptions likewise count only when recorded before their local day starts, and nothing changes after it takes effect, so a minute's calendar state is fixed before the minute; (3) catch-up after Cronz downtime: a missing queue_minute row is Unavailable (MissingMinute, NotObservedLive), every other minute Unobserved (NotObservedLive) and counted as not available, never Available from current Redis state; (4) a site with no weekly version in force is open around the clock; time zone from the site's airport (UTC without one); local dates stored with each minute; (5) calendar read with ViewSite (every role of the site), changed with EditSite (administrators).
- Screen: none. The story's wording asks for the calendar API, the job and the GET availability API; no screen is named, so this story is API only and adds no Playwright functional test (the gate's "functional tests for this story" is empty). A calendar and availability screen is deferred.
- Tests: unit `OperatingCalendarTests` (Dubai hours, overnight Friday and Sunday into Monday, whole day, versions in force, late or removed versions and exceptions, exception replacing a day but not the previous overnight part, maintenance recorded before, at and after its start, cancelled before and after, windows over closed hours, London spring forward 90 and fall back 210 minutes, 23 and 25 hour days, Beirut skipped midnight), `OperatingHoursTests` (parsing, 24:00, overlaps on the week's circle, four intervals a day, day names, JSON round trip and hostile stored JSON), `AvailabilityRuleTests` (decidable minute, live window, available, stale at 150 s and 150.001 s, ahead of the clock, lag, missing, reason order, catch-up Unobserved whatever Redis shows, ratio and grouping, settings), `SiteCalendarRulesTests` (dates, hours, reasons with invisible characters, windows started, not whole minutes, offsets, too long, too far, hostile values with fixed messages, entities), `AvailabilityJobsTests` (no HTTP client, schedule, settings at start). Integration `AvailabilityTests` (calendar service with audit, replace, refusals and 409s once in effect; site and entry scoping; ledger live Available, idempotent rerun, stale, lagging and missing reasons, Redis failure and the run-level skip, catch-up with Unobserved and Unavailable minutes and the cursor over several passes, a second replica holding the lock, calendar hours, closed exception, counted and late maintenance over three Dubai days, London fall-back day of 1,500 minutes through the read service, RunAsync, runtime privileges and hypertable, refused rows). E2E `availability.spec.ts` (calendar as a one-site administrator, markup and SQL stored as text, 409, 16 refused bodies without echo, 401, 403, 404 cross-site and cross-entry, audit; availability values per day and week from planted rows, roles, 404 cross-site before the range, 10 refused ranges without echo, 92 days accepted; the real Cronz job records an E2E site's planted live minute as Available, then Unavailable with StaleZone once its snapshots stop), eight rows in security/permission-matrix.json. E2E Cronz now gets the run's Redis and a 30-second grace.
- Docs: formulas F18 (Availability during the pilot), decisions.md, pilot-to-confirm.md TC-83, architecture overview (table and Cronz row), wiki 11 section 2b (calendar), wiki 15 section 8 (availability KPI), wiki 10 section 4.8k (runbook), Ariva.Api.Main.http.
- Gates: backend PASS (2,816 unit tests); integration PASS, 373 passed and 1 skipped (full run; the `AvailabilityTests` class, 11 tests, run again after the run-level Redis skip was added: all pass); e2e PASS, 526 passed, 1 skipped, 1 flaky (full run on fresh services; `availability.spec.ts` 8 of 8, permission matrix included); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before), self-test PASS; docs PASS; quick PASS; web not run (no web change). The flaky test is `functional/live-under-load.spec.ts` (passed on retry): it also fails alone about one run in three with Cronz's Redis switched off again, so it is not caused by this story; worth a look in its own story.
- Security review, first verdict (2026-10-07): FAIL, three required and three recommended findings, all fixed; re-review pending. Required: (1) a concurrent duplicate exception or weekly version of the same day answered 500 (unique index at commit): `SvcSiteCalendar` now flushes the insert before the audit entry, catches 23505 (`GenericADOException` with `PostgresException`), promises not to commit and answers 409 (`ExceptionTaken`; the new `WeekConcurrent`, "The version was set concurrently; read the calendar and try again", in `SiteCalendarErrors.Conflicts`); deterministic integration tests hold a raw transaction with the conflicting row and its audit entry, wait until the service's insert is blocked on it (`pg_blocking_pids`), commit, and assert the 409 error, one row and exactly one audit entry, for exceptions and weeks. (2) No test of a successful removal: the integration calendar test now removes a future week version, exception and maintenance window before the clock moves (result, gone from the view, `deleted_on` set, audit `WeekRemoved`, `ExceptionRemoved`, `MaintenanceCancelled` with their before summaries and no after summary); the E2E suite asserts 200 on each DELETE in a test of its own (the afterAll only cleans up after a failure and accepts 200 or 404), 404 on a second removal, the soft delete, and the removal actions with their before summaries in `audit_entry`. (3) CWE-501: a snapshot whose minute ends more than 60 s ahead of the job's clock (`MinuteUtc + 1 min - now > MaxAhead`) now counts as stale (StaleZone, chosen over StreamLag because such a snapshot is no evidence of liveness, as a PublishedUtc ahead already was); tests `Decide_Should_MarkTheZoneStale_When_ItsMinuteIsAheadOfTheClock` and the minute in progress at exactly the tolerance (accepted) and a tick beyond (stale). Recommended: (4) 0042 adds `CHECK (recorded_utc < starts_utc)` on `maintenance_window` (new SHA-256 faaad5c8c98fc307fba7f422a595f0db4142190baa8be8ed304ad83e72100665), with a forged-insert test expecting 23514 at and after the start; the ledger test no longer plants a late window (the calendar's own guard stays unit tested); accepted residual risk recorded in the script and decisions.md: the runtime role keeps UPDATE on calendar columns (soft delete), reaching only minutes not yet written. (5) A site whose airport's IANA id does not resolve on the Cronz host is skipped for the run (rolled back, no minutes, cursor unmoved, `AvailabilitySitePass.TimeZoneUnresolved`, `AvailabilityRun.SitesSkipped`), with one warning per site until it resolves (the stored id is not logged); a site without an airport stays on UTC; integration test with a capturing logger. (6) Counts carry `availabilityMaintenanceAsUnavailable` (available over operating plus maintenance minutes) beside `availability`; F18, wiki 15 section 8 and TC-83 say the KPI annex should cap declared maintenance minutes per month or use the stricter ratio.
- Gates after the fixes (2026-10-07): backend PASS (2,820 unit tests); integration PASS, 377 passed and 1 skipped (full run; `AvailabilityTests` 15 tests); e2e PASS, 528 passed, 1 skipped, none flaky (full run on fresh services; `availability.spec.ts` 9 of 9); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS.
- Owner decisions (Ahmad Al-Sadder, 2026-10-07), recorded in docs/product/decisions.md: the available-minute definition (every queue zone of the profile in force has a snapshot published within 150 s, its minute reached and trailing real time by at most 180 s, and a queue_minute row) accepted as the Proposed KPI-annex definition going to the client as TC-83; maintenance windows recorded at or after their start never count; a site with no calendar is open 24/7. All kept.
- Learnings: NHibernate's raw SQL takes `::int` for a named parameter (use CAST(... AS integer)); a zone of a published profile can only be inserted while the profile is a draft (trigger), so tests plant draft, zones, then publish; node-postgres needs explicit casts when one parameter appears in two type contexts.
- Security re-review (2026-10-07): verdict PASS. All six findings of the first review fixed and tested; no audit row can commit after a caught unique violation (the unit of work rolls back); maintenance windows have no unique index, so no race there. Low, carried forward: the calendar API falls back to UTC for an airport time zone the host cannot resolve while the ledger skips that site (correctness edge; consider 409 or 400 in SvcSiteCalendar); MaxFutureEntries (400) is a count-then-insert check that concurrent requests can exceed by a few (bounded, accepted).

## 2026-10-07 ARV-117a Sensor-only desk engine per site for the shadow nowcast (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary, part A (owner decision 2026-10-07): the shadow nowcast moved out of `queue_minute` into `queue_minute_shadow` (script 0043), keyed like `queue_minute` and written in the same checkpoint transaction as the published row (`StreamStore.WriteLiveAsync`: the live staging table, now with a primary key, feeds both upserts). The runtime role has INSERT, UPDATE of the value columns and SELECT of the two key columns only, no DELETE or TRUNCATE. Verified on PostgreSQL 17 with TimescaleDB 2.30 (a scratch container, then the integration tests): ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE needs SELECT on the conflict target's columns, and an `EXCLUDED.<column>` reference needs SELECT on that column of the target table (the story's expectation that EXCLUDED avoids it does not hold), so the shadow upsert sets its values from the staging table by key (`SET (...) = (SELECT ... FROM stage_queue_live s WHERE s.zone_key = queue_minute_shadow.zone_key AND ...)`). New chunks copy the hypertable's table-level privileges (not its column grants, which is less privilege); the check is made on the hypertable. 0043 copies 0041's rows (`shadow_nowcast_degraded IS NOT NULL`), drops 0041's two constraints and three columns (0041 untouched), and creates `ariva_validation_reader` (NOLOGIN, SELECT on `queue_minute_shadow` only), following 0001's role pattern; ARV-104f grants it to its own login.
- Summary, part B: `SensorOnlyDeskEngine` (Ariva.Core/Desks, pure) wraps a `DeskStateEngine` whose desks are the site's desks with staff or service zones, with those zones as their only sources; `Offer` applies `DeskZoneReading` only and refuses and counts anything else; at most 2,000 desks per site (left out in key order, counted); `Restore` also refuses any AMAN memory (session, transaction, transaction count, presence time). `DeskFeed` runs it per site beside the published engine, offers it the same zone readings, writes its minutes to `desk_sensor_minute` (script 0044: seconds per state and the F11 flag, checks per column and on the total, desk key with a site, runtime role without DELETE or TRUNCATE) in the same transaction as `desk_minute`, keeps its snapshot in `DeskFeedState.SensorEngine` (absent in older states), and rebuilds it by replaying the stored zone readings of the last 15 minutes (those the zone cursor has passed, a minute at a time, at most `MaxRead`, the latest) when the snapshot is absent, invalid, AMAN-tainted or oversized. Counted warnings name the site and a count only (desks left out, readings refused, minutes beyond 120,000 a read). `DeskTermSource` joins `desk_sensor_minute` to the desk minutes (`DeskMinuteSample.Sensor`, `DeskSensorSample`), and `DeskTerms.SensorOnly` uses it for each desk that has one (open at 30 s, flagged when the minute is flagged or 30 s Unknown), keeping ARV-117's reading for desks without one.
- Scripts: 0043_queue_minute_shadow.sql SHA-256 9cff3b10a2f061853b9c7b6962121227c85061a5ecf66c7e97e1f861d9ca752d (after the review fixes; was aaed6b14...); 0044_desk_sensor_minute.sql SHA-256 bea4098537db481def0ac767b9efe7e1c3163a3e18c945b68126e7357c22de2e (checksums.lock).
- Privilege test results (`QueueStreamTests.RuntimeLogin_Should_WriteButNeverReadTheShadow_When_TheStreamStoreUpserts`, a login made by `ariva_ensure_runtime_login`): the stream store inserts, rewrites the same minutes through ON CONFLICT and writes a minute in a second chunk; 21 statements fail with 42501 (each value column, `SELECT *`, `TABLE`, `row_to_json(s)`, `SELECT s`, a WHERE and an ORDER BY over a value, `SET x = x + 1`, RETURNING on UPDATE and INSERT, an `EXCLUDED` upsert, both COPY forms, a chunk read directly by column and by wildcard, DELETE, TRUNCATE, an UPDATE of a key); `count(*)` and `min(zone_key)` work; `has_column_privilege` and `has_table_privilege` confirm SELECT false, INSERT true, value UPDATE true, key UPDATE false, not a member of the reader role; `ariva_validation_reader` reads all three rows and cannot read `queue_minute`.
- Exposure tests: the ARV-117 per-statement wildcard and whole-row scans of `queue_minute` (the 47-statement theory and the source scan) are retired, since `queue_minute` no longer holds the shadow; the name scan is narrowed to `queue_minute_shadow` (only the store, the engine's two files and the scripts); new scans and type-graph tests keep `desk_sensor_minute`, `SensorOnlyDeskEngine` and `DeskSensorSample` to the desk feed, the store, `DeskTermSource` and `DeskTerms`, and no exposed type has a member named after them. The pg_depend test is now `Migrations_Should_LeaveNoReadPathOverTheShadowOrTheSensorOnlyDeskMinutes_When_Applied`: an exact allowlist (empty) of views, rules, materialized views, continuous aggregates, SQL functions, user triggers and publications over either table or its chunks, with a rolled-back positive control of 9 such objects, each caught (the third review's optional hardening on triggers and publications included).
- Tests: unit `SensorOnlyDeskEngineTests` (profiles, the 2,000-desk cap, a 50,000-reading flood, 50 random hours with every kind of AMAN input interleaved giving identical minutes and every AMAN input refused, an AMAN-logged-out desk Serving from its zones, exact JSON continuation, 10 hostile snapshots refused), `ShadowNowcastTests` (F8 at an all-AMAN site with AMAN live: published 7.5 and 7.89 unchanged, the shadow's term n_open = 6 from the zones; zones showing four, then none: NothingOpen; silent, flagged and NaN sensor minutes; an AMAN-only desk beside sensor desks; 400 random sets where only AMAN changes), `DeskZoneScenarioTests` (below), `ShadowNowcastExposureTests`. Integration: `QueueStreamTests` (store, restart comparison of the shadow table, runtime privileges, dependents, 8 new runtime-role cases), `ImmigrationIntakeTests` (desk term from `desk_sensor_minute`), `DeskZoneFeedTests` (A1 logged out by AMAN is Serving in the sensor-only minutes, S1 as published, the snapshot holds A1 and S1 only, an AMAN-tainted snapshot is refused and rebuilt with the same values, XS4's counter).
- Scenario (S-18 to S-20, AR-08 to AR-22, AMAN reporting every Visitors desk's staffing as live sessions): the published engine records 0 sensor-derived open desk minutes of 2,524 open desk minutes (the gap); the sensor-only engine's minutes equal a zones-only engine's exactly, within one desk of the scenario's open desks in 206 of 209 minutes (98.6 percent), 182 exact (87.1 percent), largest difference 5; the shadow's n_open equals the zones' in every minute and the published n_open is AMAN's.
- Golden replay: unchanged, 3cce63edfb17660f53a3fb7eead6c8ae92b20c491135b406a7df8cb032199a6f over 3,202 outputs (a replay has no desk term); overflow case unchanged.
- Decisions for the owner (docs/product/decisions.md entry and Open decisions row): key columns readable by the runtime role (required by the upsert); what ARV-104f's login gets beside the reader role; the Proposed bounds; the 15-minute rebuild; and, with no sensor cycle time, the shadow's number at an all-AMAN site is still the exit term while a desk is open (ARV-117a changes its desk term and its "nothing open" minutes), so the owner may want to revisit the sensor cycle time now that sensor-only open desk minutes exist.
- Docs: formulas F8, F10, F11; overview (tables and desk feed); data-boundary and wiki 14 rows for both tables; asvs-l2 V8.1.2 (now enforced by privileges) and V13.2.2; wiki 03 and wiki 04 (roles check); decisions.
- Gates: backend PASS (2,794 unit tests; the retired theory accounts for the lower count); integration PASS (386 passed, 1 skipped; full run); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS. E2E not run: no API or web change.
- Security review (2026-10-07), first verdict: FAIL, one Medium and three Low. Fixed:
  - Medium (0043's data copy never tested with data): `QueueStreamTests.Script0043_Should_CopyEveryShadowOf0041AndDropItsColumns_When_QueueMinuteHoldsShadows` applies the scripts up to 0042 through `SqlScriptRunner.ApplyAsync`, writes `queue_minute` rows with a shadow number, with a shadow reason (in another chunk) and with no shadow, applies 0043, and finds exactly the two shadows with the same values, flags and `updated_on` (to the microsecond), no row for the minute without one, every `queue_minute` row kept and no `shadow_*` column or check left; the later scripts then apply on top.
  - Low-Medium (CWE-269, the read role): `QueueStreamTests.ValidationReader_Should_HoldNothingButSelectOnTheShadow_When_TheMigrationsHaveRun` checks `pg_roles` (no login, superuser, createrole, createdb, bypassrls or replication), `pg_auth_members` (no membership), `information_schema.role_table_grants` and the ACLs of relations, columns, schemas, the database, functions and default privileges (SELECT on the shadow and its chunks, USAGE on public, nothing else). 0043 now corrects an existing role's wider attributes. Not the single `ALTER ROLE ariva_validation_reader NOLOGIN NOSUPERUSER NOCREATEDB NOCREATEROLE NOBYPASSRLS` asked for: verified on PostgreSQL 17 that a migration login without SUPERUSER (a managed server's admin) may not name SUPERUSER or BYPASSRLS at all, even to turn them off ("Only roles with the SUPERUSER attribute may change the SUPERUSER attribute"), so that statement would fail every such deployment. Instead a DO block issues one ALTER ROLE per attribute that is actually set (a no-op normally; a superuser attribute then needs a superuser, so the script fails closed), and raises when the reader is a member of another role. The correction was verified by hand on a scratch container (a role made LOGIN CREATEDB is corrected, also by a CREATEROLE migration login); it is not an integration test because the role is cluster-wide and shared by the parallel test classes.
  - Low (CWE-120, minutes per read): the cap was checked before each step and a whole step added after it. `DeskStateEngine.Advance(referenceUtc, maxRecords)` (and `SensorOnlyDeskEngine`'s) now ends the step at the caller's budget, and the desk feed passes what the cap leaves, in the catch-up and in the rebuild replay, so the bound is exact. The cap is `Border:DeskFeed:MaxSensorMinutesPerRead` (Proposed 120,000, also the maximum; 100 to 120,000). Tests: `SensorOnlyDeskEngineTests.Advance_Should_CloseNoMoreMinutesThanTheBudget_When_ItCatchesUpAfterAPause` (720 minutes in steps of at most 100, together equal to one unbudgeted catch-up) and `ImmigrationIntakeTests.DeskFeed_Should_WriteExactlyTheSensorMinutesTheCapLeaves_When_ItCatchesUpAndLogTheSiteAndACountOnly` (site XS5, cap 100: an hour of catch-up writes exactly 100 minutes, warns once with the site and the count, the rest follow on the next read with no minute missing). Hardening: `SensorOnlyDeskEngine.Restore` refuses a desk whose current state is open (Serving or Idle) without being sensor-derived, or whose open time in the minute exceeds its sensor-derived time (three new hostile snapshots; a closed or unknown sensor-only desk is legitimately not sensor-derived, so `SensorDerived == false` alone would refuse valid snapshots). The same integration test forges such a snapshot (refused, rebuilt) and proves every sensor-only warning (readings refused, minutes over the cap, the refused snapshot) carries the site and a count only, with no exception and no desk key.
  - Low (CWE-862, CWE-863, documentation): the one-bit residual (an UPDATE probe tripping `ck_queue_minute_shadow_one`, 23514, rolled back, tells whether a number or a reason is held) is named in the 0043 header and asvs-l2 V8.1.2 and recorded as accepted residual risk in docs/product/decisions.md; `QueueStreamTests.RuntimeLogin_Should_LearnOnlyWhetherANumberIsHeld_When_ItProbesTheShadowCheck` pins it (with Include Error Detail: the row detail lists only the key columns and the column the probe set, never the number, the reason or the flag). Watch item in the 0043 header: never enable chunk skipping on a value column. 0043's checksum regenerated.
  - Gates after the fixes (2026-10-07): backend PASS (2,798 unit tests; golden replay unchanged, 3cce63edfb17660f53a3fb7eead6c8ae92b20c491135b406a7df8cb032199a6f over 3,202 outputs); integration PASS, 390 passed and 1 skipped (full run); scan.mjs 0 errors (the 6 pending allowlist warnings as before); docs PASS; quick PASS.
  - Security re-review (2026-10-07): all four findings of the first review fixed and tested; one Low required fix, a test of the `Border:DeskFeed:MaxSensorMinutesPerRead` bounds (99 and 120,001 refused, 100 and 120,000 accepted), added as `DeskFeedZoneTests.Settings_Should_BoundTheSensorMinutesPerRead_When_Configured`; with it the verdict is PASS. The reviewer agreed with both deviations (conditional ALTER ROLE, because a non-superuser migration login may not name SUPERUSER on PostgreSQL 16 and later; the restore rule refusing an open desk not backed by its sensors rather than any non-sensor-derived state). Accepted: the reader role's correction path has no integration test (cluster-wide role, parallel tests). Planning note: ARV-104f reads `queue_minute_shadow` but depends only on ARV-117. Backend gate after the added test: PASS.
- Deferred: ARV-104f (the comparison and its login); the sensor cycle time for the shadow, now ARV-117b (owner decision 2026-10-07: added to backlog/prd-phase1.json at priority 8 right after ARV-117a, later priorities shifted by one, depending on ARV-117a, and a dependency of ARV-137); the retention of `desk_sensor_minute` with `desk_minute`'s (To confirm).
- Learnings: in PostgreSQL an `EXCLUDED.<column>` reference in ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE needs SELECT on that column of the target table; take the values from the staging table by key instead. The 0041 rows are copied by the script itself, so a deployment that had 0041 keeps its shadows.

## 2026-10-07 ARV-117b Sensor-derived cycle time for the shadow nowcast only (MVP, branch story/mvp-phase1; passes)
- Summary: the shadow nowcast's desk term now has a cycle time of its own, so at an all-AMAN site with AMAN live its desk term is n_open from the zones over the sensor c instead of the exit term alone. `SensorCycle` (Ariva.Core/Queueing, pure): `Window` sums the lane's sensor-only Serving seconds over the W whole minutes ending at the desk term's minute (aggregates only, `SensorBusyWindow`), refusing and counting a sensor minute that is not a number, negative, above a minute, busier than open or over a minute with its Unknown time, and treating refused and missing desk minutes as Unknown; `Compute` gives c = Serving desk minutes over the queue's exits in the same minutes, or a `SensorCycleFallback` (no desk minutes, incomplete window, desks without sensor-only minutes, Unknown above 5 percent, over the cap, no exit data, too few exits, out of bounds); `Apply` gives the shadow's term the c. `DeskTerms.Compute`/`SensorOnly` take `SensorCycleSettings` and put the busy window on the sensor-only part (`DeskTerm.SensorBusy`); `ZoneProcessor.Live` takes the queue's exits over the same minutes from its `ExitRate` and `ShadowNowcasts.Inputs(queue, desks, cycleExits, settings)` builds the shadow input (the published input is `WithDesks` of the term as given, unchanged). `ShadowNowcast.CycleMinutes` carries the c taken; `StreamStore` writes it to `queue_minute_shadow.sensor_cycle_minutes`. `DeskTermSource` reads the sensor-only minutes' five state columns, checks each row against 0044's bounds (CWE-501: left out, counted in `RefusedSensorMinutes`, warning with site and count), passes the settings (`ZoneProcessorSettings.SensorCycle`, JSON-ignored so the replay settings hash is unchanged) and warns with site and count when a lane is over the cap (`CappedSensorWindows`).
- Estimator (docs/product/decisions.md, pending the owner): the method first offered (open desk minutes over exits) collapses into the exit term (the F8 throughput identity: n_open / c is the exit rate scaled by n_open over the window's mean open desks, and idle time of a short queue lowers it); it is worse than the exit term alone on the reference evening. Built: Serving desk minutes over exits, the capacity per busy desk; it adds the desks open now and the busy share, and under saturation is still the exit rate per busy desk (no independent service speed: the sensors see no service starts). Known bias: service-zone time leaves out the walk-up gap (optimistic where the gap is long); the simulator does not model it.
- Proposed values: W 10 minutes (2 to 10, within the desk term's 15-minute read), at least 10 exits, c 0.2 to 10 minutes, Unknown share at most 5 percent, at most 4,000 desk minutes per lane and window. NothingOpen is not flagged for a cycle time it does not need; the exit-term fallback is flagged by `Nowcast.Compute` as before.
- Script 0045_shadow_sensor_cycle.sql, SHA-256 1d27ed344e6b509ff28fd4effba104784ead0aea3daa60507fe84a458f326975 (checksums.lock): `sensor_cycle_minutes double precision` on `queue_minute_shadow`, `ck_queue_minute_shadow_cycle` (0.05 to 60, this column alone, so no new probe bit), runtime role UPDATE of the column and no SELECT; 0041 to 0044 untouched.
- Scenario (reference evening, seed 9303, Ariva's own simulator, not field data; `SensorCycleScenarioTests`; Visitors desks AR-08 to AR-22 all with AMAN codes and AMAN live, S-18 to S-20 reporting their zones, 17:20 to 20:25, 186 minutes, truth the realised mean wait of the people joining in the minute after the nowcast's minute): published (AMAN n_open and lane cycle time) mean absolute error 1.17 min, bias -1.07; shadow with the sensor cycle time 0.74, +0.53 (sensor c in 151 of 186 minutes, c 1.53 to 1.76, median 1.59, against the scenario's 1.58 min service time); shadow without it (ARV-117a) 0.81, +0.61; open desk minutes over exits 1.01, +0.94. The published error is the emulator's: its AMAN interval statistics read a lane cycle time of 1.00 min all evening (60 s per transaction, at most one transaction a desk minute).
- Published unchanged: unit (500 random desk sets: identical published inputs and results with and without the busy window, its exits and other settings) and scenario (the evening's published rows, serialised as the ledger takes them, and every live snapshot identical with the sensor cycle time, without it and with the open-time estimator; the shadow differs in more than 50 minutes; the same input gives the same shadow every run).
- Golden replay: unchanged, 3cce63edfb17660f53a3fb7eead6c8ae92b20c491135b406a7df8cb032199a6f over 3,202 outputs (a replay has no desk term; the new settings are not in the settings hash and change no replay output).
- Tests: unit `SensorCycleTests` (11 table-driven F8 cases, NaN and negative inputs, flagged and Unknown minutes, missing desks and minutes, exits, cap, settings, read-back validation, Apply), `ShadowNowcastTests` (all-AMAN F8 cases: published 7.5 and 7.89, shadow 7.5 and 7.89 from the zones' n_open over the sensor c; four desks c 1.0 and 2.0; fallbacks; the 500-case published property), `SensorCycleScenarioTests`, `ShadowNowcastExposureTests` (holders of `SensorCycleResult` none, of `SensorBusyWindow` the desk term, the zone processor and the stream's desk term source and worker; no exposed member named after it; source scan of `sensor_cycle`, `SensorBusy`; 0045 in the shadow table scan; `SensorCycle.cs` in the sensor sample scan). Integration `QueueStreamTests` (the store writes and replaces the cycle time, writes an out-of-range one as none, the check refuses five forged values; runtime role: seven new 42501 statements over the column, SELECT false and UPDATE true on it; the probe test shows the column's check reveals nothing of the stored row; the dependents test's positive control adds a view and a SQL function over the column), `ImmigrationIntakeTests` (the busy window from `desk_sensor_minute`, the published term unchanged, a row planted outside 0044's bounds left out, counted and logged with site and count only, a lane over a cap of 100 desk minutes logged with site and count only).
- Docs: formulas F8 (sensor cycle time of the shadow, its table of Proposed values, the reasoning, the rules, the new test cases and the scenario figures; the shadow paragraph's cycle time and consequences rewritten) and F11; decisions.md (entry and Open decisions row); overview (queue_minute_shadow row); data-boundary and wiki 14 rows; asvs-l2 V8.1.2 (the new column; the read-back check); wiki 03.
